{"forms":[{"form":"group rings","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Given ring R with identity not equal to zero, and group G=g_1,g_2,...,g_n, the group ring RG has elements of the form a_1g_1+a_2g_2+...+a_ng_n (where a_i isin R) such that the sum of a_1g_1+a_2g_2+...+a_ng_n and b_1g_1+b_2g_2+...+b_ng_n is (a_1+b_1)g_1+(a_2+b_2)g_2+...+(a_n+b_n)g_n and the product is ∑ₖ₌₁ⁿ(∑_(g_ig_j=g_k)a_ib_j)g_k."],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"group algebra"}],"upstream_line":2306030,"upstream_offset":7131832435,"upstream_record_sha256":"e625eaed82fadaa90417c25944844a83aa4aa47e3eaeac17b841d71fd6acc021","word":"group ring"}
